- **cap**: - Always run `git branch --show-current` before any commit. If on main, STOP. - Never use `--no-verify` on git commits. - Conventional commits: `feat(&lt;crate&gt;):`, `fix(&lt;crate&gt;):`, `refactor(&lt;crate&gt;):`. - Run quality gates through `taskit`, never raw `cargo`. - Do NOT run gates by hand before committing or pushing.
